  def swap_two_values(output):
      """Swap two rows' 2026 numbers in one section: every sum still holds, so only agreement can reveal it."""
      swapped = copy.deepcopy(output)
      cash = [r for r in swapped["records"] if r["label"] == "Cash and deposits" and r["period"] == "2026-03-31"][0]
      notes = [r for r in swapped["records"] if r["label"] == "Notes receivable" and r["period"] == "2026-03-31"][0]
      for key in ("value", "raw"):
          cash[key], notes[key] = notes[key], cash[key]
      return swapped
  
  
  def test_a_tie_goes_to_text_and_cells_where_the_extractors_disagree_are_flagged_even_if_both_pass():
      text = build_output(FIXTURE_PDF)
      vision = swap_two_values(text)
      assert [c["status"] for c in vision["checks"]] == [c["status"] for c in text["checks"]]  # still all pass
      chosen = arbitrate(text=text, vision=vision)
      assert chosen["extractor"] == "text" and chosen["status"] == "ok"
      assert len(chosen["disagreements"]) == 2
      cash = [r for r in chosen["records"] if r["label"] == "Cash and deposits" and r["period"] == "2026-03-31"][0]
      assert cash["warnings"] == ["extractors_disagree: text 608,281 vs vision 26,333"]
      agreed = [r for r in chosen["records"] if r["label"] == "Land" and r["period"] == "2026-03-31"][0]
      assert agreed["warnings"] == []
  EOF

  p = "src/bsparser/arbitrate.py"

  def _flag_disagreements(winner: Dict, loser: Dict) -> None:
      """Every cell the two extractors read differently is flagged, even when both pass every check:
      two swapped rows in a section keep all sums intact, so agreement is extra evidence."""
      other = {_key(r): r for r in loser["records"]}
      winner["disagreements"] = []
      for rec in winner["records"]:
          theirs = other.get(_key(rec))
          if theirs is None or theirs["raw"] != rec["raw"]:
              seen = "missing" if theirs is None else theirs["raw"]
              rec["warnings"].append(f"extractors_disagree: {winner['extractor']} {rec['raw']} vs {loser['extractor']} {seen}")
              winner["disagreements"].append({"key": list(_key(rec)), winner["extractor"]: rec["raw"], loser["extractor"]: seen})

⏺ Bash(cat > tests/test_pipeline.py <<'EOF'
  import json
  import subprocess
  from pathlib import Path
  
  from bsparser.pipeline import parse_with_fallback
  
  FIXTURES = Path(__file__).parent.parent / "data" / "fixtures"
  CLEAN_PDF = FIXTURES / "english_balance_sheet.pdf"
  
  
  def canned_reply():
      data = json.loads((FIXTURES / "english_balance_sheet.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
      return {
          "header": [f"({data['unit_raw']})", "(Mar. 31, 2025) (Mar. 31, 2026)"],
          "rows": [{"label": r["label"], "depth": r["depth"],
                    "amounts": [v["raw"] for v in r["values"]] if r["values"] else []} for r in data["rows"]],
      }
  
  
  def make_scan(tmp_path):
      """An image-only PDF: the clean fixture page rendered to a picture, with no text layer."""
      from reportlab.lib.pagesizes import A4
      from reportlab.pdfgen import canvas
  
      stem = tmp_path / "page"
      subprocess.run(["pdftoppm", "-r", "110", "-png", "-singlefile", str(CLEAN_PDF), str(stem)], check=True)
      scan = tmp_path / "scan.pdf"
      c = canvas.Canvas(str(scan), pagesize=A4)
      c.drawImage(f"{stem}.png", 0, 0, width=A4[0], height=A4[1])
      c.save()
      return scan
  
  
  def test_a_clean_text_page_never_calls_the_vision_model():
      calls = []
      out = parse_with_fallback(CLEAN_PDF, runner=lambda cmd: calls.append(cmd))
      assert calls == []
      assert out["extractor"] == "text" and out["status"] == "ok"
      assert "alternative" not in out
  
  
  def test_an_image_only_scan_has_no_text_records_and_is_routed_to_vision(tmp_path):
      scan = make_scan(tmp_path)
      calls = []
  
      def fake_runner(cmd):
          calls.append(cmd)
          return json.dumps(canned_reply(), ensure_ascii=False)
  
      out = parse_with_fallback(scan, runner=fake_runner)
      assert len(calls) == 1
      assert out["extractor"] == "vision" and out["status"] == "ok"
      assert len(out["records"]) == 74 and len(out["checks"]) == 20
      assert out["alternative"]["extractor"] == "text" and out["alternative"]["records"] == []
      assert any("no usable text layer" in i["message"] for i in out["issues"])
  EOF
  .venv/bin/python -m pytest -q --tb=short tests/test_pipeline.py 2>&1 | tail -3
  cat > src/bsparser/pipeline.py <<'EOF'
  from typing import Dict
  
  from bsparser.arbitrate import arbitrate
  from bsparser.output import build_output, output_from_page
  from bsparser.page import parse_lines
  from bsparser.vision import _claude_runner, lines_from_vision, run_vision
  
  
  def parse_with_fallback(pdf_path, runner=_claude_runner) -> Dict:
      """D7 routing. Text first. Only a page with no usable text, or whose identity checks fail, goes to vision.
      The same checks judge both outputs."""
      text = build_output(pdf_path)
      needs_vision = not text["records"] or text["status"] != "ok"
      if not needs_vision:
          text["extractor"] = "text"
          return text
      reply = run_vision(pdf_path, runner=runner)
      vision = output_from_page(parse_lines(lines_from_vision(reply)), str(pdf_path))
      return arbitrate(text=text, vision=vision)
  EOF

  def test_any_nil_mark_is_the_same_printed_nil_but_other_marks_are_not_interchangeable():
      from bsparser.amounts import same_printed_text
  
      assert same_printed_text("－", "-") and same_printed_text("—", "－") and same_printed_text("-", "—")
      assert same_printed_text("608, 281", "608,281") and same_printed_text("６０８", "608"[:0] + "６０８")
      assert not same_printed_text("△0", "(0)")  # a zero with a mark is not another notation
      assert not same_printed_text("-", "0")  # nil is never zero
  EOF
  .venv/bin/python -m pytest -q --tb=short tests/test_amounts.py 2>&1 | tail -3
  python3 - <<'EOF'
  p = "src/bsparser/amounts.py"
  s = open(p, encoding="utf-8").read()
  s = s.replace("from dataclasses import dataclass\n", "import unicodedata\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n", 1)
  s += '''
  
  def same_printed_text(printed: str, other: str) -> bool:
      """Width and spacing are typing noise (full-width digits, spaces). Every nil mark is the same nil.
      Other marks are not noise: a triangle zero is not (0), and nil is never 0."""
      def norm(text: str) -> str:
          text = unicodedata.normalize("NFKC", text).replace(" ", "")
          return "-" if text in NIL_MARKS or text in {"\\u2014", "-"} else text
      return norm(printed) == norm(other)
  '''
  open(p, "w", encoding="utf-8").write(s)
  EOF
  .venv/bin/python -m pytest -q --tb=short 2>&1 | tail -3)

  cat > src/bsparser/vision_eval.py <<'EOF'
  import json
  import sys
  from collections import Counter
  from pathlib import Path
  
  from bsparser.arbitrate import arbitrate
  from bsparser.gold import evaluate, load_gold
  from bsparser.output import build_output, output_from_page
  from bsparser.page import parse_lines
  from bsparser.report import _share
  from bsparser.vision import lines_from_vision
  
  
  def judge_replies(pdf_path, gold_path, reply_paths) -> str:
      """Judge saved vision replies with the same page rules, identity checks and gold as the text parser,
      and count the cells where vision and the text layer read different text."""
      golds = load_gold(gold_path)
      text = build_output(pdf_path)
      lines = ["| run | status | checks passed | gold exact | cells differing from the text layer |",
               "|---|---|---|---|---|"]
      for n, path in enumerate(reply_paths, start=1):
          reply = json.loads(Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
          vision = output_from_page(parse_lines(lines_from_vision(reply)), f"vision run {n}")
          passed = sum(c["status"] == "pass" for c in vision["checks"])
          exact = sum(r.status == "exact" for r in evaluate(vision, golds))
          differing = len(arbitrate(text=text, vision=vision)["disagreements"])
          lines.append(f"| run {n} | {vision['status']} | {_share(passed, len(vision['checks'])).split(' (')[0]} "
                       f"| {_share(exact, len(golds))} | {differing} |")
      return "\n".join(lines) + "\n"
  
  
  def main(argv) -> int:
      if len(argv) < 3:
          print("usage: python -m bsparser.vision_eval <pdf> <gold template> <reply.json> [more replies]", file=sys.stderr)
          return 2
      print(judge_replies(argv[0], argv[1], argv[2:]))
      return 0
  
  
  if __name__ == "__main__":
      sys.exit(main(sys.argv[1:]))
  EOF
  .venv/bin/python -m pytest -q --tb=short 2>&1 | tail -3)

⏺ Now the docs. I'll replace the "added below once it is built" line in the report with the real results and their limits, add a decision note, and update the README.

⏺ Bash(python3 - <<'EOF'
  p = "docs/report.md"
  s = open(p, encoding="utf-8").read()
  old = "(Prototype results are added below once it is built.)\n"
  assert old in s
  new = """### Vision prototype results
  
  Built as a small prototype (`src/bsparser/vision.py`, `arbitrate.py`, `pipeline.py`). The page is rendered to a 150 dpi PNG and headless Claude Code (Read tool only, prompt on stdin, image path only, never the PDF text) transcribes it into the same JSON schema. The same page rules, identity checks and gold judge the result. Three real runs on the real Japanese page, all forced through vision (normally a clean text page never calls the model):
  
  | run | status | checks passed | gold exact | cells differing from the text layer |
  |---|---|---|---|---|
  | run 1 | ok | 20 of 20 | 74 of 74 (100%) | 0 |
  | run 2 | ok | 20 of 20 | 74 of 74 (100%) | 0 |
  | run 3 | ok | 20 of 20 | 74 of 74 (100%) | 0 |
  
  Reproduce: `python -m bsparser.vision_eval <pdf> <gold> outputs/vision_reply_japanese_run1.json ...` (saved raw replies are in `outputs/`). Every label the model returned matched the printed text exactly. The only variation across runs was the nil glyph: run 1 wrote `—` (em dash) for the two printed `－`, runs 2 and 3 kept `－`. All nil marks mean the same nil (`-`, `—`, `－`), so comparison treats them as equal, but the model does not guarantee glyph fidelity.
  
  How to read this, honestly:
  - **The strong signal is the 0 differing cells against the text layer.** Two pipelines with different inputs (text layer versus pixels) agree on all 74 cells, and the 20 identity checks pass on the vision output too.
  - **Agreement with gold is weak evidence on its own.** The gold was read from the same image by a Claude chat, so a vision model and the gold reader are correlated.
  - **Clean page only.** This PDF is a crisp digital rendering. A real scan (skew, noise, low resolution) is untested. The scan routing is tested only with a synthetic image-only PDF and a canned reply, offline.
  - **Three runs, one page.** Model output varies between runs (the dash glyph did). No claim beyond this page.
  - **Cost and speed.** About 27 seconds per page through the CLI, and it needs a logged-in Claude Code. That is prototype-grade. Production would use an API or a fine-tuned model.
  - **Bugs the real run found that offline tests could not:** the prompt was swallowed by the variadic `--add-dir` option (fixed: prompt on stdin, failures now raise with stderr), and period dates on separate lines broke the header split (fixed: header lines are joined). The nil-glyph difference also exposed that my gold comparison was stricter than the domain rule (fixed: every nil mark equals any other).
  - **Identity checks cannot see swapped rows.** That is why every cell where the two extractors differ is flagged even when both pass.
  """
  s = s.replace(old, new)
  open(p, "w", encoding="utf-8").write(s)
  
  p = "docs/decisions.md"
  s = open(p, encoding="utf-8").read()
  note = """## D16. Vision prototype (agreed, built after the text parser; details decided while building)
  - Headless Claude Code, Read tool only, prompt on stdin (the variadic `--add-dir` option swallows a positional prompt). The prompt carries the PNG path only, never the PDF or its text. Production would use an API or a fine-tuned model.
  - The reply is JSON `{header, rows: [{label, depth, amounts}]}`. It is turned into the same lines as the text parser, so roles, checks, status and warnings are shared. Header lines are joined into one line so period dates on separate lines still split header from rows.
  - Routing (`pipeline.parse_with_fallback`): a clean text page (records present, status ok) never calls the model. No records, or any status other than ok, goes to vision. Arbitration is D7: fewer failed checks wins, text wins ties, both failed returns text as failed with vision attached, no text returns vision. Disagreeing cells are flagged on the chosen records and listed in `disagreements`.
  - Every nil mark (`-`, `—`, `－`) is the same printed nil in all comparisons (gold, baseline, disagreement). Other marks stay distinct (`△0` is not `(0)`, nil is never 0).

The same page rules, identity checks and gold judge it. A clean text page never calls the model. A page with no text layer goes to vision, and a page whose checks fail also goes to vision, then arbitration picks the output. Three real runs on the real page, all forced through vision:
  
  | run | status | checks passed | gold exact | cells differing from the text layer |
  |---|---|---|---|---|
  | 1 | ok | 20 of 20 | 74 of 74 | 0 |
  | 2 | ok | 20 of 20 | 74 of 74 | 0 |
  | 3 | ok | 20 of 20 | 74 of 74 | 0 |
  
  How much weight to put on it:
  - **The strong signal:** 0 cells differ from the text layer, because two inputs (text layer and pixels) agree.
  - **Weaker signal:** agreement with gold, because gold was also a Claude reading of the same image.
  - **Not tested:** a real scan. Routing for image-only PDFs is tested only with a synthetic scan and a canned reply. Anything beyond this clean page and these three runs is not claimed.
  - **Speed:** about 27 seconds per page.
  
  Bugs that only the real run exposed:
  - **Prompt lost:** the variadic `--add-dir` option swallowed the prompt, so the first call exited with status 1. The prompt now goes on stdin, and CLI failures raise with stderr.
  - **Header split:** the model put the two period dates on separate lines, which broke the header split. Header lines are now joined.
  - **Nil glyph:** run 1 wrote an em dash where the page prints a full-width dash, and my gold comparison called that wrong. All nil marks now compare as the same nil, while `△0` still differs from `(0)` and nil still differs from 0. Before the fix this showed as 72 of 74 on run 1.

  - **Paired tests:** I paired tests twice again (in the pipeline routing tests), and one test I wrote was a guard.

  sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))  # app.py sits at the repo root

  REAL_PDF = ROOT / "data" / "balance_sheet_after_correction.pdf"
  ENGLISH_PDF = ROOT / "data" / "fixtures" / "english_balance_sheet.pdf"
  
  
  def three_page_pdf(tmp_path):
      from reportlab.lib.pagesizes import A4
      from reportlab.pdfgen import canvas
  
      path = tmp_path / "report.pdf"
      c = canvas.Canvas(str(path), pagesize=A4)
      c.drawString(60, 780, "Annual report 2025. Message from the chairman.")
      c.showPage()
      c.drawString(60, 780, "Consolidated balance sheet (in thousands of yen)")
      c.drawString(60, 760, "Total assets 7,534,110 5,135,249 Cash 2,002,429 608,281")
      c.showPage()
      c.drawString(60, 780, "Consolidated statements of cash flows 1,000 2,000")
      c.save()
      return path
  
  
  def test_the_balance_sheet_page_is_found_in_a_longer_document_and_ranked_first(tmp_path):
      candidates = find_balance_sheet_pages(three_page_pdf(tmp_path))
      assert candidates[0].page == 2
      assert "balance sheet" in candidates[0].reason.lower()
      assert [c.page for c in candidates][0] == 2
  
  
  def test_real_japanese_and_english_pages_are_found_on_page_one():
      assert find_balance_sheet_pages(REAL_PDF)[0].page == 1
      assert find_balance_sheet_pages(ENGLISH_PDF)[0].page == 1
  
  
  def test_a_document_with_no_balance_sheet_gives_no_candidates(tmp_path):
      from reportlab.lib.pagesizes import A4
      from reportlab.pdfgen import canvas
  
      path = tmp_path / "other.pdf"
      c = canvas.Canvas(str(path), pagesize=A4)
      c.drawString(60, 780, "Meeting minutes. Agenda item one.")
      c.save()
      assert find_balance_sheet_pages(path) == []
  
  
  def test_extract_page_writes_a_one_page_pdf_with_that_pages_text(tmp_path):
      import pdfplumber
  
      out = extract_page(three_page_pdf(tmp_path), 2, tmp_path / "page2.pdf")
      with pdfplumber.open(out) as pdf:
          assert len(pdf.pages) == 1
          assert "Total assets" in pdf.pages[0].extract_text()
  EOF
  .venv/bin/python -m pytest -q --tb=short tests/test_app.py 2>&1 | tail -3)

  def test_the_page_renders_status_table_checks_and_downloads_for_an_uploaded_pdf():
      from streamlit.testing.v1 import AppTest
  
      def script(pdf_path):
          import sys
          from pathlib import Path
  
          import streamlit as st
  
          sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(pdf_path).parent.parent))
          import app
  
          class Upload:
              name = Path(pdf_path).name
  
              def getvalue(self):
                  return Path(pdf_path).read_bytes()
  
          st.file_uploader = lambda *args, **kwargs: Upload()
          app.main()
  
      at = AppTest.from_function(script, args=(str(REAL_PDF),), default_timeout=60).run()

⏺ The reviewer's disagreement is now stated plainly in the report, and the Streamlit page is built and committed. `data/heldout/` doesn't exist yet, so I haven't run anything on your PDFs. 109 tests pass.
  
  **Reviewer disagreement:** the report now says what it was and that the reason is unverified. The output has role `item` for 貸倒引当金. The reviewer wrote that the gold template says `contra`. The current template says `item` and has since D12, so the output matches the file as it stands. Where the reviewer got `contra` is an inference, and I wrote it down as one.
  
  **The page** is `app.py`, a single file at the repo root, and the parser is used as is. Start it with `.venv/bin/streamlit run app.py`.
  - **Drop in a PDF:** it scores every page for balance-sheet keywords (貸借対照表, 資産の部, 資産合計, balance sheet, total assets) and for how many grouped numbers the page has. You get a dropdown of candidate pages, best first. If nothing looks like a balance sheet, you can pick a page number yourself.
  - **Parsing:** it extracts that one page and parses it by text layer.
  - **What it shows:**
    - the status with a one-line explanation
    - unit, periods and row counts
    - the table: indented labels, the English reading label, role, and the printed value per period
    - the checks, each with difference, bound and a pass, FAILED or skipped result
    - the issues
    - buttons for the records CSV and the full JSON
  
  What I tested:
  - **Page finder:** a longer 3-page document finds page 2, the real and English pages are found on page 1, and a document with no balance sheet gives no candidates.
  - **Extraction and display:** one-page extraction and the display table are covered.
  - **Rendering and server:** the full page renders without an exception for the real PDF, and the headless server answers HTTP 200.
  - **Not covered:** I haven't clicked through it in a real browser. That's the part you'll test.
  
  The usual slips:
  - **Test batching:** I wrote four finder tests at once.
  - **Wrong test count:** one expectation of mine was wrong, and I fixed it in my new test. The real page has one issue, the printed page number, so three tables render and not two.
  - **Streamlit warning:** a deprecation warning led me to change `use_container_width` to `width="stretch"`.
  
  Limits to know before you test:
  - **No scan fallback:** the page doesn't use the vision fallback. A scanned page will show "no table rows found".
  - **Page picking:** the finder picks one page, so a balance sheet that spans two pages (assets and liabilities separately) shows only one.
  
  Put your PDFs in `data/heldout/` and tell me. I'll run each one through the same steps the page uses (find the page, extract it, parse it) and show you what happens for every file before changing any code.

⏺ Bash(SP=/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-teja-Desktop-ur-ai-parser-setup/a3e0201a-6f55-434c-9bf7-ac47dda76ff4/scratchpad && cat > $SP/run_heldout.py <<'EOF'
  import sys, json, tempfile
  from collections import Counter
  from pathlib import Path
  sys.path.insert(0, ".")
  from app import extract_page, find_balance_sheet_pages
  from bsparser.output import build_output
  for pdf in sorted(Path("data/heldout").glob("*.pdf")):
      print("=" * 100)
      cands = find_balance_sheet_pages(pdf)
      print(pdf.name, "| candidates:", [(c.page, round(c.score, 1)) for c in cands[:6]])
      if not cands:
          print("  NO CANDIDATE PAGE"); continue
      best = cands[0]
      print("  best:", best.page, best.reason)
      work = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp())
      one = extract_page(pdf, best.page, work / "p.pdf")
      out = build_output(one)
      cc = Counter(c["status"] for c in out["checks"])
      print(f"  status={out['status']} unit={out['unit']!r} ({out['unit_raw']!r}) periods={out['periods']} rows={len(out['rows'])} records={len(out['records'])} checks={dict(cc)}")
      for i in out["issues"][:12]:
          print("   issue:", i["kind"], i["row_id"], i["message"][:110])
      if len(out["issues"]) > 12: print("   ... issues total", len(out["issues"]))
      for c in out["checks"]:
          if c["status"] != "pass":
              print("   check:", c["status"], c["label"], c["period"], "diff", c["difference"], "bound", c["bound"], c["reason"][:60])
      print("  first rows:", [(r["level"], r["role"], r["label"]) for r in out["rows"][:6]])
      json.dump(out, open(f"{sys.argv[1]}/{pdf.stem}_p{best.page}.json", "w", encoding="utf-8"), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1)
  EOF
  mkdir -p $SP/heldout_before && .venv/bin/python -I $SP/run_heldout.py $SP/heldout_before 2>&1 | head -120)

⏺ Bash(SP=/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-teja-Desktop-ur-ai-parser-setup/a3e0201a-6f55-434c-9bf7-ac47dda76ff4/scratchpad && mkdir -p scripts outputs/heldout_before && cp $SP/heldout_before/*.json outputs/heldout_before/ && cat > scripts/run_heldout.py <<'EOF'
  """Run every PDF in data/heldout the way the Streamlit page does: find the best balance sheet page,
  extract it, parse it with the parser unchanged, print status, checks and issues, save the JSON.
  
  Run from the repo root: .venv/bin/python scripts/run_heldout.py <out_dir>
  """
  import json
  import sys
  import tempfile
  from collections import Counter
  from pathlib import Path
  
  sys.path.insert(0, ".")
  from app import extract_page, find_balance_sheet_pages  # noqa: E402
  from bsparser.output import build_output  # noqa: E402
  
  out_dir = Path(sys.argv[1])
  out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
  for pdf in sorted(Path("data/heldout").glob("*.pdf")):
      candidates = find_balance_sheet_pages(pdf)
      print("=" * 100)
      print(pdf.name, "| candidates:", [(c.page, round(c.score, 1)) for c in candidates[:6]])
      if not candidates:
          print("  NO CANDIDATE PAGE")
          continue
      best = candidates[0]
      one_page = extract_page(pdf, best.page, Path(tempfile.mkdtemp()) / "page.pdf")
      out = build_output(one_page)
      counts = Counter(c["status"] for c in out["checks"])
      print(f"  page {best.page}: status={out['status']} unit={out['unit']} periods={out['periods']} "
            f"rows={len(out['rows'])} records={len(out['records'])} checks={dict(counts)}")
      for issue in out["issues"]:
          print("   issue:", issue["kind"], issue["row_id"], issue["message"][:110])
      for check in out["checks"]:
          if check["status"] != "pass":
              print("   check:", check["status"], check["label"], check["period"], "diff", check["difference"], "bound", check["bound"])
      (out_dir / f"{pdf.stem}_p{best.page}.json").write_text(json.dumps(out, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1), encoding="utf-8")
  EOF
  python3 - <<'EOF'

Lines such as 有形固定資産 carry their own value and have indented detail lines below them: they are typed `item` and the sums handle them by path. A few rows were compared with the image by eye and matched. No gold, so cell accuracy is unmeasured. The finder tied pages 5 and 14 (score 8.0) and chose 5 by page order. |
  
  What the four runs show:
  - **The page finder chose the consolidated balance sheet page in all four files**, confirmed by looking at the page images.
  - **Two structures the parser does not handle, both visible in this table.** An aggregate accumulated-depreciation line at item level (two files), and net lines that stand alone with no gross row (one file). Both give false identity failures, because the lines are excluded from the sum.

  ❓ **Q18 - Accumulated depreciation printed as one line for the whole section**: In files 2 and 3 the page prints the gross lines (建物, 機械装置, ...), then a single 減価償却累計額 line at the same indent, then the subtotal. That one line applies to all the gross lines above it, so it can't link to a single gross row. I suggest:
  - it keeps role `contra`;
  - `parent` stays empty, because D2 allows a parent only for a single gross link, and today the parser wrongly gives it the section header;
  - it counts as an ordinary contributor to the section subtotal, so the sum becomes gross lines plus that negative line;
  - it is not flagged as an issue, because it is a normal layout;
  - there is no gross-plus-contra-equals-net check for it, because no net line exists.
  
  ➡️ Yes. Role `contra`, no parent, counted in the section sum, no issue raised.

⏺ The stray root copy is deleted, and the four PDFs are committed under `data/heldout/`. D17 records the aggregate-contra decision (role `contra`, no parent, counted in the section sum, no issue). It also records your rounding facts as read from the cover pages by you, not by me. The report now says the file 1 difference of 5 is the cut-off and not an extraction error. D4's strict bound assumes rounding, so on these filings it can raise a false failure.
  
  Next is the tolerance itself.
  
  ❓ **Q19 - How the tolerance learns the rounding mode, and the bound for a cut-off**: The balance sheet page never says how its numbers were rounded, and that fact sits on another page. So I suggest the parser never guesses it and takes it as an explicit per-document setting with three values:
  - **`round`:** today's strict bound, `floor(0.5 × (items + 1))`.
  - **`cut` (切り捨て):** the bound is the number of items counted. Each printed item and the printed total is off by less than 1, and a cut toward zero flips the error's sign on negative lines, so the safe two-sided limit is `|difference| ≤ items`. File 1's current assets (7 items, difference 5) then passes, while a difference of 8 would still fail. This is deliberately looser than needed, since every difference we saw is zero or positive.
  - **`unstated`:** the default. It uses the `round` bound as today. The output says "rounding: unstated, strict bound used", so a reader knows the tolerance is an assumption. This is where 2452 belongs.
  
  The setting goes into the output (`rounding`) and into the command line. The Streamlit page gets a dropdown, with a hint that scans the first pages for 切り捨て or 切捨て, shows the matching sentence, and preselects `cut`, which you can override. The parser itself never reads the cover.
  
  ➡️ Yes. Three values (`round`, `cut`, `unstated`), bound `items` for `cut`, default `unstated`. The setting is recorded in the output and the page offers a hint from the cover text.
  
  After this I'll list the remaining held-out fixes (net lines with no gross row, the `-5-` page number, the thin bank page) for one last round of answers. Then I'll ask you to confirm the spec before any code.

╔══ YOU ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
║ Yes. One addition: use the same cover-page hint in the held-out script
║ and the command line, not only in the app, and record where the setting
║ came from (from the cover sentence, chosen by the user, or unstated).
║ Otherwise the "after" table would depend on someone picking the right
║ option by hand.
╚═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

──── CLAUDE ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  note = """## D18. Rounding mode of the identity tolerance (agreed)
  - A per-document setting `rounding` with three values. `round`: the strict bound floor(0.5 x (items + 1)) (D4). `cut` (切り捨て): the bound is the number of items counted, two-sided, because each printed item and the printed total is off by less than 1 and a cut toward zero flips the error sign on negative lines. `unstated`: the default, uses the `round` bound, and the output says "rounding: unstated, strict bound used".

  ❓ **Q20 - `（純額）` lines that stand alone**: In file 1 the page prints 建物及び構築物（純額）, 機械装置及び運搬具（純額）, リース資産（純額） and その他（純額） directly under the 有形固定資産 header, with no gross line and no accumulated-depreciation line anywhere. They are the only numbers printed for those assets, so they are really ordinary items. Today they are typed `net`, can't find a gross row, raise four `net_label_mismatch` issues, and are left out of the section sum. That leaves two false failures with differences of 6,005 and 5,399. Same principle as D12: `net` and `contra` are reserved for lines that have a gross row on the page. I suggest:
  - a line gets `net` only when the nearest shallower row is a gross item and the label is that item's label plus the net suffix, as today;
  - a net-suffix line whose nearest shallower row is a header (or a total) is a plain `item`, with no parent and no issue, because it is a normal layout;
  - a net-suffix line whose nearest shallower row is a gross item but with a different label stays flagged `net_label_mismatch` and stays unlinked, as D? decided earlier for that case.
  
  ➡️ Yes. `net` only when linked to a gross item above with a matching label. A stand-alone net-suffix line directly under a header is an `item` with no issue. A real label mismatch is still flagged.

  ❓ **Q21 - Page numbers**: The parser drops a line with no label (like `- 4 -` or a bare `4`), because the extractor reads the amount-like tokens and leaves no label. But `-5-` has no spaces, so it fails the amount test, stays as label text, and ends up as a bogus row with an `irregular_indent` issue (file 2). I suggest a label-only line is treated as a page number when both of these hold:
  - it is the last line of the table region, below every real row, and
  - after width normalisation it is only a page number: a number alone, a number between dashes with or without spaces (`-5-`, `- 5 -`, full-width forms), or `Page 5` or `5/12`.
  
  It is ignored the same way as today, with an `ignored_line` issue saying it looks like a page number. That issue is informational and doesn't change the status. A row in the middle of the table that happens to look like a number is never dropped.
  
  ➡️ Yes. Only the last line, only a pure page-number pattern, ignored with an informational `ignored_line` issue.

  import sys, tempfile, traceback, json
  from collections import Counter
  from pathlib import Path
  sys.path.insert(0, ".")
  from app import extract_page
  from bsparser.output import build_output
  jobs = [("140120260416505142.pdf", 7), ("140120260416505142.pdf", 11),
          ("140120260513529466.pdf", 8),
          ("140120260514535920.pdf", 7), ("140120260514535920.pdf", 10),
          ("tanshin202603.pdf", 6)]
  out_dir = Path(sys.argv[1]); out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
  for name, page in jobs:
      print("=" * 100); print(name, "page", page)
      one = extract_page(Path("data/heldout") / name, page, Path(tempfile.mkdtemp()) / "p.pdf")
      try:
          o = build_output(one)
      except Exception as exc:
          tb = traceback.format_exc().strip().splitlines()
          print("  CRASH:", type(exc).__name__, exc); print("   ", tb[-3].strip()); print("   ", tb[-2].strip()[:140]); continue
      cc = Counter(c["status"] for c in o["checks"])
      print(f"  status={o['status']} unit={o['unit']} periods={o['periods']} rows={len(o['rows'])} records={len(o['records'])} checks={dict(cc)}")
      for i in o["issues"][:8]: print("   issue:", i["kind"], i["row_id"], i["message"][:100])
      for c in o["checks"]:
          if c["status"] != "pass": print("   check:", c["status"], c["label"], c["period"], "diff", c["difference"], "bound", c["bound"], c["reason"][:50])
      print("  rows:", [(r["level"], r["role"], r["label"][:14]) for r in o["rows"][:4]], "...", [(r["level"], r["role"], r["label"][:14]) for r in o["rows"][-3:]])
      json.dump(o, open(out_dir / f"{Path(name).stem}_p{page}.json", "w", encoding="utf-8"), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1)
  EOF

⏺ Bash(SP=/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-teja-Desktop-ur-ai-parser-setup/a3e0201a-6f55-434c-9bf7-ac47dda76ff4/scratchpad && .venv/bin/python -I - <<'EOF'
  import sys, tempfile, unicodedata
  sys.path.insert(0, ".")
  from pathlib import Path
  from app import extract_page
  from bsparser.pdf_lines import extract_lines
  from bsparser.page import parse_lines, _split_header
  def look(name, page, only_odd=True):
      one = extract_page(Path("data/heldout")/name, page, Path(tempfile.mkdtemp())/"p.pdf")
      lines = extract_lines(one)
      header, table = _split_header(lines)
      pg = parse_lines(lines)
      print("====", name, "page", page, "| periods", pg.periods, "| header lines", len(header), "| rows", len(pg.rows))
      for r in pg.rows:
          bad = (not r.values and r.role not in ("header",)) or any(a.kind != "value" for a in r.values) or (r.values and len(r.values) != len(pg.periods))
          if bad:
              print("  ", r.id, r.role, repr(r.label[:30]), [(a.value, a.kind, a.raw, [hex(ord(c)) for c in a.raw if not c.isdigit() and c != ","]) for a in r.values])
  look("140120260513529466.pdf", 8)
  look("140120260416505142.pdf", 11)
  look("140120260514535920.pdf", 10)
  EOF)

  - **The statement of changes in equity is not a balance sheet.** Its wide matrix layout is out of scope for this parser. It must at least not crash.

⏺ Bash(cat > scripts/run_pages.py <<'EOF'
  """Run chosen pages of the held-out PDFs as is (parser unchanged), catching crashes and showing them.
  
  Run from the repo root:
    .venv/bin/python scripts/run_pages.py <out_dir> 140120260416505142.pdf:7 tanshin202603.pdf:6 ...
  """
  import json
  import sys
  import tempfile
  import traceback
  from collections import Counter
  from pathlib import Path
  
  sys.path.insert(0, ".")
  from app import extract_page  # noqa: E402
  from bsparser.output import build_output  # noqa: E402
  
  out_dir = Path(sys.argv[1])
  out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
  for spec in sys.argv[2:]:
      name, page = spec.rsplit(":", 1)
      print("=" * 100)
      print(name, "page", page)
      one_page = extract_page(Path("data/heldout") / name, int(page), Path(tempfile.mkdtemp()) / "page.pdf")
      try:
          out = build_output(one_page)
      except Exception as exc:  # shown, never hidden: a crash is a finding
          trace = traceback.format_exc().strip().splitlines()
          print("  CRASH:", type(exc).__name__, exc)
          print("   ", trace[-3].strip())
          print("   ", trace[-2].strip()[:140])
          continue
      counts = Counter(c["status"] for c in out["checks"])
      print(f"  status={out['status']} unit={out['unit']} periods={out['periods']} "
            f"rows={len(out['rows'])} records={len(out['records'])} checks={dict(counts)}")
      for issue in out["issues"]:
          print("   issue:", issue["kind"], issue["row_id"], issue["message"][:100])
      for check in out["checks"]:
          if check["status"] != "pass":
              print("   check:", check["status"], check["label"], check["period"], "diff", check["difference"], "bound", check["bound"])
      (out_dir / f"{Path(name).stem}_p{page}.json").write_text(json.dumps(out, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1), encoding="utf-8")
  EOF
  python3 - <<'EOF'

  note = """## D21. Held-out fixes, full list (PROPOSED: waiting for the owner to confirm before any code)
  Already agreed and only restated here: D17 aggregate contra line; D18 rounding setting (`round`, `cut`, `unstated`) with the shared cover hint and a recorded source; D19 net-suffix lines (stand-alone ones are items with a note); D20 page numbers (last line, pure pattern only).
  Also agreed (Q22): a group check. An `item` that has its own value and indented detail lines directly below it must equal the sum of those detail lines, with the same bound, status and skip rules. Detail lines introduced by うち, (うち or "of which" are not components: no check, and a note. The group line stays an `item` with no `parent`. Seen on the bank page: 有形固定資産 and 無形固定資産.
  
  From the other pages (the owner's findings):
  1. Never crash. Any check that cannot be computed becomes `skipped` with a reason, never an exception: a printed total that is nil (4098 page 11, 2452 page 10), a row with fewer values than period columns (4224 page 8), any missing value. As a safety net, an exception raised inside one check becomes a skipped check carrying the error text, and the page still returns its other results.
  2. A total with nothing to add up (total liabilities and net assets, 4098 p7, 2452 p7, bank p6) is never `failed` for lack of components. It is skipped with a reason, or better, checked as total liabilities plus total net assets (item 5).
  3. A subtotal with no heading above it adds only what comes since the previous subtotal (bank page 6). The section's final total then adds the earlier subtotals plus any items not covered by one. Note: for 純資産の部合計 that means 株主資本合計 + その他の包括利益累計額合計 + 非支配株主持分, not only the items since the last subtotal. Headed sections are unchanged.
  4. Total assets must equal total liabilities plus net assets (holds on all four companies, per the owner).
  
  Added by Claude while tracing the causes, to be confirmed:
  5. Nil marks: add the long horizontal bar U+2015 (the cause of the 4224 page 8 crash), the en dash U+2013 and the minus sign U+2212 when they stand alone. They are nil, like hyphen, em dash and full-width dash. Before digits, behaviour is unchanged.
  6. Total labels are recognised by exact printed wording, never by `label_en`: total assets (資産合計, 資産の部合計, Total assets); total liabilities (負債合計, 負債の部合計, Total liabilities); total net assets (純資産合計, 純資産の部合計, Total net assets, Total equity); total liabilities and net assets (負債純資産合計, 負債及び純資産合計, 負債及び純資産の部合計, Total liabilities and net assets, Total liabilities and equity). Note 純資産合計 contains 資産合計, so matching is on the whole label. Unlisted wording means the cross checks are skipped with a reason, never guessed.
  7. The statement of changes in equity (4098 p11, 2452 p10) is a matrix of 5 to 8 amount columns and is out of scope. It must not crash. It will report its column-count issues as today.
  
  Proposed design for items 2 and 4, because the pieces sit on different pages (assets | liabilities and net assets; for the bank, assets and liabilities | net assets):
  - The parser stays page by page and unchanged. The page, the held-out script and the command line gather consecutive pages into one statement: after the assets page, include the next pages (up to 2) that have the same unit and periods and carry liabilities or net assets headings or totals.
  - Statement-level checks, per period: (a) total assets = total liabilities and net assets, EXACT (bound 0, because the two printed numbers are the same amount printed twice and round or cut to the same integer); (b) total liabilities and net assets = total liabilities + total net assets, with the bound for two items under the rounding setting (round 1, cut 2). The two pieces may come from different pages. If a piece is not found the check is skipped and the reason names it.
  - Statement status is the worst of the page statuses and the cross checks (unreadable over failed over ok). The JSON gets a `statement` object (pages, cross checks, status); the per-page outputs keep their shape.
  - On a single page, a total whose components are not all there is skipped with that reason; the statement-level check does the real work.
  
  """
  s = s.replace("## D20. Page numbers (agreed)", note + "## D20. Page numbers (agreed)", 1)

  ⎿  from dataclasses import dataclass
     from typing import List, Optional
     
     from bsparser.page import Page
     
     
     @dataclass
     class Check:
         label: str  # the printed total the check judges
         row_id: str
         period: str
         expected: Optional[int]  # sum of the contributing items; None when skipped
         printed: Optional[int]
         difference: Optional[int]  # printed minus expected
         bound: int
         items: int  # contributors that carry a rounded number
         status: str  # pass | failed | skipped
         reason: str = ""
     
     
     def allowed_bound(items: int) -> int:
         """Strict bound (D4): each item and the printed total are rounded, so 0.5 x (items + 1), floored."""
         return (items + 1) // 2
     
     
     def _status(difference: int, bound: int) -> str:
         return "failed" if abs(difference) > bound else "pass"
     
     
     def run_checks(page: Page) -> List[Check]:
         by_id = {r.id: r for r in page.rows}
         checks = []
         for row in page.rows:
             if row.role != "net" or row.parent is None:
                 continue
             contra = [r for r in page.rows if r.role == "contra" and r.parent == row.parent]
             gross = by_id[row.parent]
             for p, period in enumerate(page.periods):
                 unreadable = [r for r in [gross, row] + contra if r.values[p].kind == "unreadable"]
                 if unreadable:
                     ids = ", ".join(r.id for r in unreadable)
                     checks.append(Check(row.label, row.id, period, None, None, None, 0, 0, "skipped",
                                         f"unreadable value in {ids}"))
                     continue
                 expected = gross.values[p].value + sum(c.values[p].value or 0 for c in contra)
                 printed = row.values[p].value
                 checks.append(Check(row.label, row.id, period, expected, printed, printed - expected,
                                     allowed_bound(2), 2,
                                     _status(printed - expected, allowed_bound(2))))
         for row in page.rows:
             if row.role not in ("subtotal", "total"):
                 continue
             contributors = []
             for r in page.rows:
                 if r.role == "item" and r.values and r.path == row.path:
                     nets = [n for n in page.rows if n.role == "net" and n.parent == r.id]
                     contributors.append(nets[0] if nets else r)
                 elif (r.role == "subtotal" and len(r.path) == len(row.path) + 1
                       and r.path[:len(row.path)] == row.path):
                     contributors.append(r)  # a sub-section total feeds the total above it
             for p, period in enumerate(page.periods):
                 unreadable = [r for r in contributors + [row] if r.values[p].kind == "unreadable"]
                 if unreadable:
                     ids = ", ".join(r.id for r in unreadable)
                     checks.append(Check(row.label, row.id, period, None, None, None, 0, 0, "skipped",
                                         f"unreadable value in {ids}"))
                     continue
                 amounts = [c.values[p].value for c in contributors if c.values[p].value is not None]
                 expected = sum(amounts)
                 printed = row.values[p].value
                 checks.append(Check(row.label, row.id, period, expected, printed, printed - expected,
                                     allowed_bound(len(amounts)), len(amounts),
                                     _status(printed - expected, allowed_bound(len(amounts)))))
         return checks
     [This command modified 1 file you've previously read: docs/decisions.md. Call Read before editing.]

  def test_a_printed_total_that_is_nil_is_skipped_with_a_reason_and_never_crashes():
      from bsparser.page import parse_lines
      from bsparser.pdf_lines import Line
  
      page = parse_lines([
          Line("(in thousands of yen)", 400.0, 70.0),
          Line("(Mar. 31, 2025) (Mar. 31, 2026)", 300.0, 94.0),
          Line("Assets", 60.0, 120.0),
          Line("Current assets", 72.0, 134.0),
          Line("Cash", 84.0, 148.0, ["100", "100"]),
          Line("Notes receivable", 84.0, 162.0, ["200", "200"]),
          Line("Total current assets", 84.0, 176.0, ["－", "300"]),  # nil total in 2025
      ])
      checks = run_checks(page)
      nil_total = check_for(checks, "Total current assets", "2025-03-31")
      assert nil_total.status == "skipped" and "printed total is nil" in nil_total.reason
      assert (nil_total.expected, nil_total.printed, nil_total.difference) == (None, None, None)
      assert check_for(checks, "Total current assets", "2026-03-31").status == "pass"
  EOF

  def test_rows_with_fewer_values_than_periods_skip_the_checks_they_touch_instead_of_crashing():
      from bsparser.page import parse_lines
      from bsparser.pdf_lines import Line
  
      def page_with(notes, total):
          return parse_lines([
              Line("(in thousands of yen)", 400.0, 70.0),

      """A reason when any of these rows has a different number of values than the page has period columns."""
      odd = [r for r in rows if len(r.values) != len(page.periods)]
      return "; ".join(f"row {r.id} has {len(r.values)} value{'s' if len(r.values) != 1 else ''} "

  def test_an_unexpected_error_inside_one_check_becomes_a_skipped_check_with_the_error_text(monkeypatch):
      import bsparser.checks as checks_module
  
      def boom(items):
          raise RuntimeError("boom")
  
      monkeypatch.setattr(checks_module, "allowed_bound", boom)
      checks = run_checks(parse_page(FIXTURE_PDF))  # must not raise
      assert len(checks) == 20  # every check is still reported
      assert {c.status for c in checks} == {"skipped"}
      assert all("RuntimeError: boom" in c.reason for c in checks)
  EOF
  .venv/bin/python -m pytest -q --tb=short tests/test_checks.py 2>&1 | tail -4
  python3 - <<'EOF'
  p = "src/bsparser/checks.py"
  s = open(p, encoding="utf-8").read()
  i = s.index("def run_checks(page: Page) -> List[Check]:")
  head = s[:i]
  body = s[i:]
  new_body = '''def _net_checks(page: Page, row, by_id) -> List[Check]:
      checks: List[Check] = []
      contra = [r for r in page.rows if r.role == "contra" and r.parent == row.parent]
      gross = by_id[row.parent]
      reason = _wrong_width([gross, row] + contra, page)
      if reason:
          return _skip_all(page, row, reason)
      for p, period in enumerate(page.periods):
          unreadable = [r for r in [gross, row] + contra if r.values[p].kind == "unreadable"]
          if unreadable:
              ids = ", ".join(r.id for r in unreadable)
              checks.append(Check(row.label, row.id, period, None, None, None, 0, 0, "skipped",
                                  f"unreadable value in {ids}"))
              continue
          printed = row.values[p].value
          if printed is None or gross.values[p].value is None:
              nil = row if printed is None else gross
              checks.append(Check(row.label, row.id, period, None, None, None, 0, 0, "skipped",
                                  f"value is nil in {nil.id}"))
              continue
          expected = gross.values[p].value + sum(c.values[p].value or 0 for c in contra)
          checks.append(Check(row.label, row.id, period, expected, printed, printed - expected,
                              allowed_bound(2), 2, _status(printed - expected, allowed_bound(2))))
      return checks
  
  
  def _total_checks(page: Page, row) -> List[Check]:
      checks: List[Check] = []
      contributors = []
      for r in page.rows:
          if r.role == "item" and r.values and r.path == row.path:
              nets = [n for n in page.rows if n.role == "net" and n.parent == r.id]
              contributors.append(nets[0] if nets else r)
          elif (r.role == "subtotal" and len(r.path) == len(row.path) + 1
                and r.path[:len(row.path)] == row.path):
              contributors.append(r)  # a sub-section total feeds the total above it
      reason = _wrong_width(contributors + [row], page)
      if reason:
          return _skip_all(page, row, reason)
      for p, period in enumerate(page.periods):
          unreadable = [r for r in contributors + [row] if r.values[p].kind == "unreadable"]
          if unreadable:
              ids = ", ".join(r.id for r in unreadable)
              checks.append(Check(row.label, row.id, period, None, None, None, 0, 0, "skipped",
                                  f"unreadable value in {ids}"))
              continue
          printed = row.values[p].value
          if printed is None:
              checks.append(Check(row.label, row.id, period, None, None, None, 0, 0, "skipped",
                                  f"printed total is nil in {row.id}"))
              continue
          amounts = [c.values[p].value for c in contributors if c.values[p].value is not None]
          expected = sum(amounts)
          checks.append(Check(row.label, row.id, period, expected, printed, printed - expected,
                              allowed_bound(len(amounts)), len(amounts),
                              _status(printed - expected, allowed_bound(len(amounts)))))
      return checks
  
  
  def _guarded(page: Page, row, make) -> List[Check]:
      """A check that fails unexpectedly is reported as skipped with the error, so one bad row never costs the page."""
      try:
          return make()
      except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - the point is to never crash
          return _skip_all(page, row, f"check could not run: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")
  
  
  def run_checks(page: Page) -> List[Check]:
      by_id = {r.id: r for r in page.rows}
      checks: List[Check] = []
      for row in page.rows:
          if row.role == "net" and row.parent is not None:
              checks.extend(_guarded(page, row, lambda row=row: _net_checks(page, row, by_id)))
      for row in page.rows:
          if row.role in ("subtotal", "total"):
              checks.extend(_guarded(page, row, lambda row=row: _total_checks(page, row)))
      return checks
  '''
  open(p, "w", encoding="utf-8").write(head + new_body)
  EOF
  .venv/bin/python -m pytest -q --tb=short 2>&1 | tail -3)

  def aggregate_page():

          Line("(2025年３月31日) (2026年３月31日)", 300.0, 94.0),

      ])
  
  
  def test_an_aggregate_contra_line_has_no_parent_and_no_issue():

      assert contra.role == "contra"
      assert contra.parent is None  # D2: parent is only a single gross link
      assert page.issues == []
  
  
  def test_an_aggregate_contra_line_counts_in_the_section_subtotal_and_no_net_check_exists():

      assert [c.label for c in checks] == ["有形固定資産合計", "有形固定資産合計"]  # no gross-plus-contra check

      assert (current.expected, current.difference, current.status) == (2400, 0, "pass")
  EOF

  import re
  app = open("app.py", encoding="utf-8").read()
  start = app.index("AMOUNT = re.compile")

  block = app[start:end]
  pages = '''"""Find the balance sheet page(s) of a PDF and cut one page out, so the page parser (page 1 only) is used unchanged."""
  import re
  import subprocess

  def seven_item_page(printed_prior):
      from bsparser.page import parse_lines
      from bsparser.pdf_lines import Line
  
      items = [Line(f"item {n}", 84.0, 148.0 + 14 * n, ["100", "100"]) for n in range(7)]
      return parse_lines([
          Line("（単位：百万円）", 400.0, 70.0),
          Line("(2025年３月31日) (2026年３月31日)", 300.0, 94.0),
          Line("資産の部", 60.0, 120.0),
          Line("流動資産", 72.0, 134.0),
          *items,
          Line("流動資産合計", 84.0, 148.0 + 14 * 7, [printed_prior, "704"]),
      ])
  
  
  def test_the_same_difference_fails_under_unstated_and_passes_under_cut():
      from bsparser.checks import run_checks
  
      page = seven_item_page("705")  # items sum to 700: difference 5 in 2025 and 4 in 2026
      unstated = run_checks(page)  # default is unstated
      assert [(c.difference, c.bound, c.status) for c in unstated] == [(5, 4, "failed"), (4, 4, "pass")]
      cut = run_checks(page, rounding="cut")
      assert [(c.difference, c.bound, c.status) for c in cut] == [(5, 7, "pass"), (4, 7, "pass")]
      over = run_checks(seven_item_page("708"), rounding="cut")  # difference 8 is still beyond the cut bound
      assert [(c.difference, c.bound, c.status) for c in over][0] == (8, 7, "failed")
  EOF

  cat > src/bsparser/rounding.py <<'EOF'
  import re
  import unicodedata
  from dataclasses import dataclass
  
  import pdfplumber
  
  COVER_PAGES = 3  # the cut-off sentence sits on the cover, not on the balance sheet page
  CUT_OFF = re.compile(r"(百万円|千円|円)未満[^)\n]*?切(?:り)?捨て")
  UNIT_OF = {"百万円": "million_yen", "千円": "thousand_yen", "円": "yen"}
  
  
  @dataclass
  class Hint:
      mode: str  # cut | unstated
      source: str  # cover_sentence | unstated
      sentence: str = ""
      note: str = ""
  
  
  def find_rounding_hint(pdf_path, unit) -> Hint:
      """D18. A cut-off sentence on the first pages counts only when the unit it names is the balance sheet's unit.
      The parser never reads the cover otherwise and never guesses."""
      with pdfplumber.open(pdf_path) as pdf:
          text = "\n".join(unicodedata.normalize("NFKC", page.extract_text() or "") for page in pdf.pages[:COVER_PAGES])
      found = [(m.group(0), UNIT_OF[m.group(1)]) for m in CUT_OFF.finditer(text)]
      for sentence, sentence_unit in found:
          if sentence_unit == unit:
              return Hint("cut", "cover_sentence", sentence)
      if found:
          sentence, sentence_unit = found[0]
          return Hint("unstated", "unstated", sentence,
                      f"the cover says {sentence!r} (cut-off in {sentence_unit}) but the balance sheet is in {unit}: "
                      "rounding is not stated for this unit")
      return Hint("unstated", "unstated", "", "no cut-off sentence found on the first pages")
  EOF
  .venv/bin/python -m pytest -q --tb=short 2>&1 | tail -3)

  def output_from_page(page, source: str, rounding: str = "unstated", rounding_source: str = "unstated",
                       rounding_sentence: str = "", rounding_note: str = "") -> Dict:
      checks = run_checks(page, rounding)
      if rounding == "unstated" and not rounding_note:
          rounding_note = "rounding: unstated, strict bound used"

          "source": source,
          "status": page_status(page, checks),
          "rounding": rounding,
          "rounding_source": rounding_source,
          "rounding_sentence": rounding_sentence,
          "rounding_note": rounding_note,'''

  from pathlib import Path
  
  from bsparser.analyse import analyse
  
  HELDOUT = Path(__file__).parent.parent / "data" / "heldout"
  
  
  def check_of(out, label, period):
      return [c for c in out["checks"] if c["label"] == label and c["period"] == period][0]
  
  
  def test_auto_finds_the_page_and_takes_the_rounding_mode_from_the_cover_and_says_so():
      out = analyse(HELDOUT / "140120260416505142.pdf")  # 4098: cover says 百万円未満切捨て, balance sheet in 百万円
      assert out["page"] == 6 and out["pages_total"] == 14

      current_assets = check_of(out, "流動資産合計", "2025-03-31")

  def test_an_explicit_choice_is_recorded_as_user_and_unit_mismatch_stays_unstated():
      chosen = analyse(HELDOUT / "140120260416505142.pdf", rounding="round")
      assert (chosen["rounding"], chosen["rounding_source"]) == ("round", "user")
      assert check_of(chosen, "流動資産合計", "2025-03-31")["status"] == "failed"  # the strict bound is 4
      mismatch = analyse(HELDOUT / "140120260514535920.pdf")  # 2452: cut-off stated in millions, sheet in thousands

  .venv/bin/python -m pytest -q --tb=short tests/test_analyse.py 2>&1 | tail -3
  cat > src/bsparser/analyse.py <<'EOF'
  import tempfile
  from pathlib import Path
  from typing import Dict, Optional
  
  import pdfplumber
  
  from bsparser.output import output_from_page
  from bsparser.page import parse_page
  from bsparser.pages import extract_page, find_balance_sheet_pages
  from bsparser.rounding import find_rounding_hint
  
  ROUNDING_CHOICES = ("auto", "round", "cut", "unstated")
  
  
  def analyse(pdf_path, page: Optional[int] = None, rounding: str = "auto") -> Dict:
      """One path for the command line, the held-out script and the page, so no result depends on hand-picked options.
      Finds the balance sheet page (unless given), parses it with the parser unchanged, and sets the rounding mode:
      `auto` takes the cover hint (D18), an explicit value is recorded as chosen by the user."""
      with pdfplumber.open(pdf_path) as pdf:
          pages_total = len(pdf.pages)
      if page is None:
          candidates = find_balance_sheet_pages(pdf_path)
          page = candidates[0].page if candidates else 1
      one_page = extract_page(pdf_path, page, Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="bsparser_")) / "page.pdf")
      parsed = parse_page(one_page)
      if rounding == "auto":
          hint = find_rounding_hint(pdf_path, parsed.unit)
          mode, source, sentence, note = hint.mode, hint.source, hint.sentence, hint.note
      else:
          mode, source, sentence, note = rounding, "user", "", ""
      out = output_from_page(parsed, str(pdf_path), rounding=mode, rounding_source=source,
                             rounding_sentence=sentence, rounding_note=note)
      out["page"], out["pages_total"] = page, pages_total
      return out
  EOF
  .venv/bin/python -m pytest -q --tb=short 2>&1 | tail -3)

  import json
  import sys
  
  from bsparser.analyse import ROUNDING_CHOICES, analyse
  from bsparser.output import write_outputs
  
  
  def main(argv) -> int:
      parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="python -m bsparser", description="Parse the balance sheet page of a PDF.")
      parser.add_argument("pdf")
      parser.add_argument("out_dir")
      parser.add_argument("--page", type=int, default=None, help="page to parse (default: the best balance sheet page)")

                          help="auto takes the cover hint; an explicit value is recorded as chosen by the user")

      saved = json.loads(json_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
      print(f"page: {saved['page']} of {saved['pages_total']}  status: {saved['status']}  issues: {len(saved['issues'])}  "
            f"checks: {len(saved['checks'])}  records: {len(saved['records'])}")
      print(f"rounding: {saved['rounding']} ({saved['rounding_source']}) {saved['rounding_sentence'] or saved['rounding_note']}".rstrip())
      print(f"wrote {json_path} and {csv_path}")
      return 0
  
  
  if __name__ == "__main__":
      sys.exit(main(sys.argv[1:]))
  ''')

  HELDOUT_4098 = ROOT / "data" / "heldout" / "140120260416505142.pdf"
  
  
  def _app_for(pdf_path):
      from streamlit.testing.v1 import AppTest
  
      def script(pdf_path):
          import sys
          from pathlib import Path
  
          import streamlit as st
  
          sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(pdf_path).parent.parent.parent))
          import app
  
          class Upload:
              name = Path(pdf_path).name
  
              def getvalue(self):
                  return Path(pdf_path).read_bytes()
  
          st.file_uploader = lambda *args, **kwargs: Upload()
          app.main()
  
      return AppTest.from_function(script, args=(str(pdf_path),), default_timeout=90)
  
  
  def test_the_page_shows_the_rounding_hint_and_lets_the_user_override_it_and_records_that():
      at = _app_for(HELDOUT_4098).run()
      assert not at.exception
      rounding = [m.value for m in at.info if m.value.startswith("Rounding:")]
      assert len(rounding) == 1 and "cut" in rounding[0] and "cover_sentence" in rounding[0]
      assert "百万円未満切捨て" in rounding[0]
      assert at.subheader[0].value == "Status: ok"  # 4098 passes under the cover's cut-off bound
      options = at.selectbox[1].options
      assert options[0].startswith("auto") and set(options[1:]) == {"round", "cut", "unstated"}
      at.selectbox[1].select("round").run()
      chosen = [m.value for m in at.info if m.value.startswith("Rounding:")][0]
      assert "round" in chosen and "chosen by you" in chosen
      assert at.subheader[0].value == "Status: failed"  # the strict bound fails the cut-off difference of 5
  EOF

⏺ Bash(sed -n '/^def build_page/,/^    odd_levels/p' src/bsparser/page.py | head -60; grep -n "class Row" -A 12 src/bsparser/page.py | head -16)

  ⎿  def build_page(lines: List[Line]) -> Page:
         lines, merged_at = _merge_wrapped(lines)
         levels = assign_levels([line.x0 for line in lines], LEVEL_TOLERANCE)
         rows: List[Row] = []
         issues: List[Issue] = []
         stack: List[Row] = []  # nearest preceding row at each shallower level
         for n, (line, level) in enumerate(zip(lines, levels), start=1):
             while stack and stack[-1].level >= level:
                 stack.pop()
             role = classify_role(line.label, has_values=bool(line.amounts), level=level)
             row = Row(id=f"r{n}", label=line.label, level=level, role=role, path=[r.label for r in stack],
                       values=[parse_amount(a) for a in line.amounts])
             if role in ("contra", "net") and stack:
                 gross = stack[-1]
                 if role == "net" and not _is_gross_plus_net(gross.label, row.label):
                     issues.append(Issue("net_label_mismatch", row.id,
                                         f"net label {row.label!r} is not {gross.label!r} plus a net suffix; not linked"))
                 elif role == "contra" and gross.role != "item":
                     pass  # one line for the whole section (D17): no single gross row, so no parent
                 else:
                     row.parent = gross.id
             rows.append(row)
             stack.append(row)
         odd_levels = set(irregular_levels([line.x0 for line in lines], LEVEL_TOLERANCE))
     17:class Row:
     18-    id: str
     19-    label: str
     20-    level: int
     21-    role: str
     22-    path: List[str]
     23-    parent: Optional[str] = None
     24-    values: List[Amount] = field(default_factory=list)
     25-
     26-
     27-@dataclass
     28-class Issue:
     29-    kind: str

  import pytest
  
  from bsparser.page import parse_lines
  from bsparser.pdf_lines import Line
  
  
  def page_with_last(label):
      return parse_lines([
          Line("（単位：百万円）", 400.0, 70.0),
          Line("(2025年３月31日) (2026年３月31日)", 300.0, 94.0),
          Line("資産の部", 60.0, 120.0),
          Line("流動資産", 72.0, 134.0),
          Line("現金及び預金", 84.0, 148.0, ["100", "200"]),
          Line(label, 286.0, 800.0),
      ])
  
  
  @pytest.mark.parametrize("label", ["-5-", "- 5 -", "－５－", "5", "Page 5", "5/12", "p.5"])
  def test_a_last_label_only_line_that_is_a_page_number_is_left_out_with_an_informational_issue(label):
      page = page_with_last(label)
      assert [r.label for r in page.rows] == ["資産の部", "流動資産", "現金及び預金"]
      assert [(i.kind, "page number" in i.message) for i in page.issues] == [("ignored_line", True)]
  
  
  def test_a_line_that_only_looks_like_a_number_in_the_middle_of_the_table_is_never_dropped():
      page = parse_lines([
          Line("（単位：百万円）", 400.0, 70.0),
          Line("(2025年３月31日) (2026年３月31日)", 300.0, 94.0),
          Line("資産の部", 60.0, 120.0),
          Line("12", 72.0, 134.0),  # a label that looks like a number, with a row after it
          Line("現金及び預金", 84.0, 148.0, ["100", "200"]),
      ])
      assert [r.label for r in page.rows] == ["資産の部", "12", "現金及び預金"]
  
  
  def test_a_last_line_that_is_not_a_page_number_stays_a_row():
      page = page_with_last("注記")
      assert [r.label for r in page.rows][-1] == "注記"
  EOF

  p = "src/bsparser/checks.py"
  s = open(p, encoding="utf-8").read()
  s = s.replace('''def _guarded(page: Page, row, make)''', '''def _group_checks(page: Page, row, rounding: str) -> List[Check]:
      """Q22: an item that has its own value and indented detail lines directly below it equals the sum of them."""
      children = [r for r in page.rows if r.role == "item" and r.values and r.path == row.path + [row.label]]
      if not children:
          return []
      reason = _wrong_width(children + [row], page)
      if reason:
          return _skip_all(page, row, reason)
      checks: List[Check] = []
      for p, period in enumerate(page.periods):
          unreadable = [r for r in children + [row] if r.values[p].kind == "unreadable"]
          if unreadable:
              ids = ", ".join(r.id for r in unreadable)
              checks.append(Check(row.label, row.id, period, None, None, None, 0, 0, "skipped", f"unreadable value in {ids}"))
              continue
          printed = row.values[p].value
          if printed is None:
              checks.append(Check(row.label, row.id, period, None, None, None, 0, 0, "skipped", f"printed total is nil in {row.id}"))
              continue
          amounts = [c.values[p].value for c in children if c.values[p].value is not None]
          expected = sum(amounts)
          checks.append(Check(row.label, row.id, period, expected, printed, printed - expected,
                              bound_for(len(amounts), rounding), len(amounts),
                              _status(printed - expected, bound_for(len(amounts), rounding))))
      return checks
  
  
  def _guarded(page: Page, row, make)''', 1)

              checks.extend(_guarded(page, row, lambda row=row: _total_checks(page, row, rounding)))
      for row in page.rows:
          if row.role == "item" and row.values:
              checks.extend(_guarded(page, row, lambda row=row: _group_checks(page, row, rounding)))
      return checks''', 1)

  import unicodedata
  from typing import Optional
  
  # D21 item 6: exact printed wording only, never label_en. 純資産合計 contains 資産合計, so the whole label is matched.
  TOTALS = {
      "assets": ["資産合計", "資産の部合計", "total assets"],
      "liabilities": ["負債合計", "負債の部合計", "total liabilities"],
      "net_assets": ["純資産合計", "純資産の部合計", "total net assets", "total equity"],
      "liabilities_net_assets": ["負債純資産合計", "負債及び純資産合計", "負債及び純資産の部合計",
                                 "total liabilities and net assets", "total liabilities and equity"],
  }
  _LOOKUP = {wording: kind for kind, words in TOTALS.items() for wording in words}
  
  
  def kind_of_total(label: str) -> Optional[str]:
      text = " ".join(unicodedata.normalize("NFKC", label).split()).lower()
      return _LOOKUP.get(text)
  EOF

  p = "src/bsparser/checks.py"
  s = open(p, encoding="utf-8").read()
  s = s.replace("from bsparser.page import Page\n", "from bsparser.page import Page\nfrom bsparser.totals import kind_of_total\n", 1)
  s = s.replace('''def _total_checks(page: Page, row, rounding: str) -> List[Check]:
      checks: List[Check] = []
  ''', '''def _sum_of_totals(page: Page, row, parts, rounding: str) -> List[Check]:
      """A total that is the sum of other printed totals (total liabilities and net assets = liabilities + net assets)."""
      checks: List[Check] = []
      reason = _wrong_width(parts + [row], page)
      if reason:
          return _skip_all(page, row, reason)
      for p, period in enumerate(page.periods):
          unreadable = [r for r in parts + [row] if r.values[p].kind == "unreadable"]
          nil = [r for r in parts + [row] if r.values[p].value is None and r.values[p].kind != "unreadable"]
          if unreadable or nil:
              ids = ", ".join(r.id for r in (unreadable or nil))
              checks.append(Check(row.label, row.id, period, None, None, None, 0, 0, "skipped",
                                  f"{'unreadable' if unreadable else 'nil'} value in {ids}"))
              continue
          expected = sum(r.values[p].value for r in parts)
          printed = row.values[p].value
          checks.append(Check(row.label, row.id, period, expected, printed, printed - expected,
                              bound_for(len(parts), rounding), len(parts),
                              _status(printed - expected, bound_for(len(parts), rounding))))
      return checks
  
  
  def _la_na_checks(page: Page, row, rounding: str) -> List[Check]:
      pieces = {kind: [r for r in page.rows if r.values and kind_of_total(r.label) == kind] for kind in ("liabilities", "net_assets")}
      missing = [name for name, kind in (("liabilities", "liabilities"), ("net assets", "net_assets")) if not pieces[kind]]
      if missing:
          return _skip_all(page, row, "total " + " and total ".join(missing) + " not on this page")
      return _sum_of_totals(page, row, [pieces["liabilities"][0], pieces["net_assets"][0]], rounding)
  
  
  def _total_checks(page: Page, row, rounding: str) -> List[Check]:
      if kind_of_total(row.label) == "liabilities_net_assets":
          return _la_na_checks(page, row, rounding)
      checks: List[Check] = []
  ''', 1)

      def row(label, a, top):

  def test_an_intermediate_subtotal_adds_only_the_items_since_the_previous_subtotal():
      checks = {c.label: c for c in run_checks(equity_page()) if c.period == "2025-03-31"}
      a = checks["株主資本合計"]

  p = "src/bsparser/checks.py"
  s = open(p, encoding="utf-8").read()
  old = '''    checks: List[Check] = []
      contributors = []
      for r in page.rows:
          if r.role == "item" and r.values and r.path == row.path:
              nets = [n for n in page.rows if n.role == "net" and n.parent == r.id]
              contributors.append(nets[0] if nets else r)
          elif r.role == "contra" and r.parent is None and r.values and r.path == row.path:
              contributors.append(r)  # an aggregate contra line is an ordinary contributor (D17)
          elif (r.role == "subtotal" and len(r.path) == len(row.path) + 1
                and r.path[:len(row.path)] == row.path):
              contributors.append(r)  # a sub-section total feeds the total above it
  '''
  new = '''    checks: List[Check] = []
      contributors = _contributors(page, row)
  '''
  assert old in s
  s = s.replace(old, new, 1)
  s = s.replace('''def _sum_of_totals(''', '''def _contributors(page: Page, row):
      """What a subtotal or total adds up. Walking the section in page order: items (a net line replaces its gross),
      aggregate contra lines and sub-section totals go into the current run. A subtotal printed with no heading above
      it adds only the run since the previous subtotal. The section's last total adds the earlier subtotals plus the
      run not yet covered by one (D21 item 3)."""
      section_totals = [r for r in page.rows if r.role in ("subtotal", "total") and r.values and r.path == row.path]
      is_last = bool(section_totals) and section_totals[-1] is row
      covered, run = [], []
      for r in page.rows[:page.rows.index(row)]:
          if r in section_totals:
              covered.append(r)
              run = []
          elif r.role == "item" and r.values and r.path == row.path:
              nets = [n for n in page.rows if n.role == "net" and n.parent == r.id]
              run.append(nets[0] if nets else r)
          elif r.role == "contra" and r.parent is None and r.values and r.path == row.path:
              run.append(r)  # an aggregate contra line is an ordinary contributor (D17)
          elif r.role == "subtotal" and len(r.path) == len(row.path) + 1 and r.path[:len(row.path)] == row.path:
              run.append(r)  # a sub-section total feeds the total above it
      return covered + run if is_last else run
  
  
  def _sum_of_totals(''', 1)
  open(p, "w", encoding="utf-8").write(s)
  EOF

  from bsparser.statement import cross_checks
  
  
  def record(label, period, value, kind="value", raw=None):
      return {"label": label, "period": period, "value": value, "kind": kind,
              "raw": raw if raw is not None else ("" if value is None else f"{value:,}")}
  
  
  def page(*records, periods=("2025-03-31", "2026-03-31")):
      return {"records": list(records), "periods": list(periods)}
  
  
  def both(label, a, b):
      return [record(label, "2025-03-31", a), record(label, "2026-03-31", b)]
  
  
  def test_total_assets_must_equal_total_liabilities_and_net_assets_exactly():
      assets = page(*both("資産合計", 13992, 13731))
      liabilities = page(*both("負債純資産合計", 13992, 13732))
      checks = cross_checks([assets, liabilities], rounding="unstated")
      a = [c for c in checks if c["label"] == "資産合計 = 負債純資産合計"]
      assert [(c["period"], c["expected"], c["printed"], c["difference"], c["bound"], c["status"]) for c in a] == [
          ("2025-03-31", 13992, 13992, 0, 0, "pass"),
          ("2026-03-31", 13731, 13732, 1, 0, "failed"),  # one off is a failure: the same amount is printed twice
      ]
  EOF

⏺ Bash(cat > src/bsparser/statement.py <<'EOF'
  from typing import Dict, List, Optional
  
  from bsparser.checks import bound_for
  from bsparser.totals import kind_of_total
  
  A_LABEL = "total assets = total liabilities and net assets"
  B_LABEL = "total liabilities and net assets = total liabilities + total net assets"
  
  
  def _find(pages: List[Dict], kind: str, period: str) -> Optional[Dict]:
      for page in pages:
          for rec in page["records"]:
              if rec["period"] == period and kind_of_total(rec["label"]) == kind:
                  return rec
      return None
  
  
  def _skipped(label: str, period: str, reason: str) -> Dict:
      return {"label": label, "period": period, "expected": None, "printed": None, "difference": None,
              "bound": 0, "items": 0, "status": "skipped", "reason": reason}
  
  
  def _usable(rec: Optional[Dict], name: str) -> Optional[str]:
      """None when the record can be used; otherwise the reason it cannot."""
      if rec is None:
          return f"{name} not found on any page"
      if rec["kind"] == "unreadable":
          return f"{name} is unreadable"
      if rec["value"] is None:
          return f"{name} is nil"
      return None
  
  
  def cross_checks(pages: List[Dict], rounding: str = "unstated") -> List[Dict]:
      """D21 item 4, Q23. The pieces may sit on different pages.
      (a) total assets = total liabilities and net assets, exactly: it is the same amount printed twice, so it rounds
          or is cut to the same integer. (b) total liabilities and net assets = total liabilities + total net assets,
          with the bound for two items. A missing piece skips the check and the reason names it."""
      periods = []
      for page in pages:
          for period in page["periods"]:
              if period not in periods:
                  periods.append(period)
      checks: List[Dict] = []
      for period in periods:
          assets, lna = _find(pages, "assets", period), _find(pages, "liabilities_net_assets", period)
          reason = _usable(assets, "total assets") or _usable(lna, "total liabilities and net assets")
          if reason:
              checks.append(_skipped(A_LABEL, period, reason))
          else:
              diff = lna["value"] - assets["value"]
              checks.append({"label": f"{assets['label']} = {lna['label']}", "period": period, "expected": assets["value"],
                             "printed": lna["value"], "difference": diff, "bound": 0, "items": 1,
                             "status": "pass" if diff == 0 else "failed", "reason": ""})
      return checks
  EOF
  .venv/bin/python -m pytest -q --tb=short tests/test_statement.py 2>&1 | tail -3)

  def test_a_missing_or_nil_or_unreadable_piece_skips_check_a_and_the_reason_names_it():

          ("2025-03-31", 6561710, 6561710, 0, 2, 2, "pass"),
          ("2026-03-31", 6653407, 6653408, 1, 2, 2, "pass"),
      ]
      assert all(c["status"] == "pass" for c in checks)  # check (a) holds as well
  
  
  def test_check_b_is_skipped_naming_the_missing_total_and_failed_beyond_its_bound():
      page6 = page(*both("純資産の部合計", 310724, 330501), *both("負債及び純資産の部合計", 6561710, 6653408))

      assert [c["status"] for c in skipped] == ["skipped", "skipped"]
      assert "total liabilities not found" in skipped[0]["reason"]

      failed = [c for c in cross_checks([off], rounding="unstated") if "+" in c["label"]]
      assert (failed[0]["difference"], failed[0]["bound"], failed[0]["status"]) == (4, 1, "failed")
  EOF
